Theorem 222.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Under the convergence analysis assumption on probability structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 840. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 552 with relations in degree 4.

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the probability space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 840 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 552. QED.
